South Marine is clipped at the bottom of the overview and Marpole at the bottom of the west frame.
- **Mission (7 areas, new `mission.json`):** Hatzic, Mission BC, Mission-West, Stave Falls, Steelhead, Durieu, Dewdney Deroche. Stave Falls, Steelhead, Durieu and Dewdney Deroche are cut at the top of the frame (about 49.28 N). Hemlock is not drawn (Hemlock Valley is off the screen). Dewdney Deroche gives way to the existing Lake Errock outline (they overlapped by about 8 km2; the Mission frames suggest Lake Errock runs about 500 m too far south along Hwy 7, to be checked when new frames arrive).
- **Abbotsford (+4, additive):** Bradner, Matsqui, Sumas Mountain, Sumas Prairie. Mt Lehman has no polygon of its own on the layer (it is printed inside Bradner). The existing five are untouched; against the screenshots the biggest misses are Abbotsford East's north-west edge (about 0.8 km2 into Matsqui) and Poplar's east edge (about 300 m too far east below Marshall Rd).

All four are cut flat at the top of the overview frame (about 49.30 N); they are whole rural watersheds, so they are large. The existing 13 areas sit within about 100 to 220 m of the screenshot polygons, mostly shifted west and north (not redrawn).
- **Coquitlam (+3, partial):** Coquitlam West, Central Coquitlam and Maillardville are drawn only where the screenshot shows them (their west slices; the east parts are on no frame). Coquitlam East is not drawn: the polygon labelled CE on the Town Centre sheet sits among Port Coquitlam streets, so `GVR-code-names.csv` probably has CE wrong (likely Central Port Coquitlam). East Richmond (72) and Gilmore (70) are drawn in `build_richmond_more_outlines.py` but held out of `richmond.json` (the `HELD` set) because the site has no page for either; Gilmore's identification is the least certain. **D-123 (2026-11-05): real privacy policy, with the Google wording Jared approved (D-103).** The old `/privacy.html` was the archived generic boilerplate ("I/we", "personal account") with a generated layer of research notes on top ("Condo buildings in Privacy" and similar). `tools/write_privacy_page.py` replaces both with a plain-language policy under BC's PIPA (who is responsible; what is collected; why; who sees it; the approved Google Maps line, unchanged; cookies; choices and rights; OIPC complaint route; changes) and clears the generated layer (`status: draft`). It states only what the site does today: forms with Cloudflare Turnstile, the vendor-hosted MyRealPage searches, Google Maps/Places/Routes loaded in the visitor's browser, no analytics or ad pixels in the code. **Jared to confirm before launch:** the privacy contact (him, email and phone as shown); that Keyvera/email/D1 handling is as described; that no analytics or ad pixels will be added (if they are, add a line); retention wording (kept as long as needed and as the law and brokerage record rules require); whether he wants a brokerage privacy officer named. Not legal advice. Build 1,674 pages; 99 tests pass.

**D-128 (2026-11-05): Private preview on Cloudflare.** Jared connected the GitHub repo (`jaredgibbons-ai/jaredgibbons-ca`, `main`) in Cloudflare Workers Builds (build `npm run build`, deploy `npx wrangler deploy`); no API token was needed. It deploys to `https://jaredgibbons-ca.jaredgibbons.workers.dev`, still `PREVIEW=true` (noindex), DNS untouched, nothing public. Locked with Cloudflare Access: Zero Trust Free plan activated by Jared (it asks for a card or PayPal, $0 due; Claude does not enter payment details), Worker policy scope **All traffic** (production and per-version preview URLs) with the policy **Cloudflare account members** only; checked that the address redirects to the Access login. To let someone else in (Hernando), add them under Zero Trust > Access. The contact form will not send email until the email setup at launch. The preview address was added to the Maps key's website list by Claude. Still Jared's step: set `PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_KEY` in the Worker's build variables and retry the build (docs/google-maps-key-setup.md). Every push to `main` now redeploys the preview automatically.

## D-140 (2026-11-05): Pre-launch audit fixes (audit dated Nov 6, 2026)
**Done in code**
- **Worker, fail-safe noindex:** `x-robots-tag: noindex, nofollow` is now sent on every answer (pages, redirects, 404s, `/api/*`, `/listing-photos/*`) unless the `INDEXABLE` variable is exactly the string `"true"`. A missing, mistyped or inherited value (`true`, `TRUE`, `1`) keeps the site closed (tested). `wrangler.jsonc` ships `INDEXABLE: "false"`; the old `PREVIEW` variable is gone. **Launch needs three things together, with Jared's approval:** `INDEXABLE="true"`, a build with `PUBLIC_PREVIEW=false` (meta robots, robots.txt, banner), and nothing else.
- **Security headers on every answer:** `x-frame-options: SAMEORIGIN`, `content-security-policy: frame-ancestors 'self'; base-uri 'self'`, `permissions-policy` (camera, microphone, payment, usb off), `strict-transport-security: max-age=31536000`. A script allowlist CSP is not added: it needs the launch inventory of third-party scripts (Google Maps, Turnstile, MyRealPage, YouTube) and should start as Report-Only.
- **Redirects (`tools/build_rules.py`, `src/worker/route.js`):** the 23 `/recip.html/listing.*` targets now go to `/recip.html` (they cannot resolve); redirects send `cache-control: public, max-age=3600`; archive redirects keep campaign parameters (`?utm_...`); `/<page>.html/<other-page>.html` (doubled relative-link prefix, six rows the crawl recorded as 403) now redirects to `/<other-page>.html` when it exists, instead of 404; trailing slash on a nested listing or blog URL redirects instead of 404; `/404.html` asked for directly now answers 404; `/blog.html/blog.feed` is 410; `/bc-property-transfer-tax-calculator.html` 301s to `/first-time-buyer-calculator.html`; `/langley-condos-under-700k.html` goes to `/langley-condos-for-sale.html` (it went down to under-499k). The crawler's probe row, the dead `hostRules` table and the wrong row count are gone (`rows_in_code` accounts for the 81 rows); `upload_source_maps: false`.
- **Forms:** honeypot field renamed `_hp_url` (autofill fills `website`).
- **Components:** `.explore`, mega-menu region labels, footer headings and legal text, form fields, `/buying` and `/selling` footers use the tokens; `--star` darkened `#a67c00` to `#8a6600` (AA); hamburger inherits the brand font; default share image `/img/og-default.jpg` (1200x630) and `summary_large_image` on all pages; lightbox image has width and height; logo is `loading="eager"` on purpose (above the fold).
- **Brokerage name** now in the footer of every page ("REALTOR® with Royal LePage Little Oak Realty"), which covers Contact, Reviews, Data & Methodology and Subscribe. "Royal LePage Little Oak" without "Realty" is completed everywhere.
- **Wording standard (build-time, `src/integrations/fix-links.mjs`, a stated exception to "archive wording untouched"):** "$500M+ in career sales" (was "More than $500M in sales" / "$500M+ in sales"), "10 straight years in the FVREB Medallion Club" (was "10 consecutive years"). 223 pages.
- **Links:** `/contact`-style extensionless links and `/mylistings.html/...` links now point straight at their final URL (180 rewritten).
- **Other:** GlenBrooke North H1 (`seo-overrides.json`); BreadcrumbList on `/fort-langley.html` and `/reviews.html`; sitemap `<lastmod>` (`src/integrations/lastmod.mjs`, dates change only when a page's `<main>` changes; run the build with `LASTMOD_WRITE=1` before committing so the manifest `src/data/lastmod.json` matches); stat tiles on Selling/Buying have a space between number and label.

**Left on purpose or for Jared**
- **Forms deliver nothing yet (audit #3).** Needs `TURNSTILE_SECRET`, a lead destination (D1, webhook or email), `ALLOWED_ORIGINS`, then one real test on the live domain. I cannot do this.
- **`/esplanade-grande-waterstone-condo-for-sale/`** stays 404 until Jared decides (OI-10: the archive lists both a redirect and a 404).
- **`/photo-credits.html`** is out of the sitemap on purpose (utility page, noindex at launch).
- **`/ladner-mobile-homes.html`, `/tsawwassen-mobile-homes.html`** have no mobile-home page to point at, so they keep going to the city homes page.
- **`/index.html` and `/`** both answer 200 with the canonical set to `/`; not redirected because every internal brand link uses `/index.html`.
- **Buying and Selling** use their own section system on purpose (they follow the live editorial design); colours use the shared tokens.
- **Design system:** the build uses Lato body and gold `#8a6600`; the older Cormorant Garamond and `#c9a96e` are retired. This is the current system.
- **Not yet done:** titles over 60 characters (353; 15 over 70), "95 Google reviews / 5.0" still hard-coded (waits on the reviews feed, BL-03), the seven 403 rows are now handled by the doubled-prefix rule but the other 404 rows were not re-checked against the old site, and the three third-party phone numbers on guide pages need Jared's confirmation.

**D-141 (2026-11-05): Everything centered.** The page container was already centered, but the reading column inside it (`.pg`, 42rem) sat against the left edge, the breadcrumb trail lost its auto margins (`.crumbs ol{margin:0}` overrode `.wrap`) and own-listing pages used a full-width container, so content started at the left gutter. Now the reading column is centered (`margin-inline:auto`), breadcrumbs share the header's container, and listing pages are a centered 68rem column. Checked left and right gaps are equal at 390, 1440 and 1920 px on guides, home, Selling and a listing; no horizontal scroll.

**D-142 (2026-11-05): Menus scroll.** The narrow dropdowns (Open Houses, Farms and the other single-column panels) had no height limit, so on a short screen the bottom ran off the page and could not be reached (the header is sticky, so scrolling the page does not help). Every dropdown panel is now capped at the space below the header and scrolls inside itself (`overscroll-behavior: contain`); in the mobile drawer panels keep expanding and the drawer scrolls. Tested at 1440x700, 1920x900 and 1301x600 (all seven panels end inside the window) and in the drawer at 1000x600.

**D-143 (2026-11-05): Search pages: compact heading, white band.** On the 600+ pages whose main content is a MyRealPage search (`idx-embeds.json`), the heading block used a 3-line display H1 and a full-height cream band, so the first listing sat about 700 px down on a laptop and the white search box looked pasted onto cream. Now: the H1 is one line (2.5rem max), left-aligned with the page container, the intro sits directly under it, and the search band is white. Pages with an unconnected listing slot get the same white band.
- **Not ours to change:** the search is a vendor iframe, so we cannot style inside it. It repeats the page's intro sentence ("Browse every townhome ... below") and shows a "Login with Facebook / Google / X, Signup, Login" bar. Both are MyRealPage saved-search settings (search description; hide the sign-in toolbar). Asked Jared to have Jerry clear them.

## D-144 — Titles, H1s and meta descriptions rewritten for every public page (2026-11-05)
Audit first: all 1,671 built pages already had one H1, a title and a description, none duplicated. Quality was the gap: 75 descriptions cut off mid-word and 60 ended in "…"; the same description sentence reused across 78, 74 and 38 pages; "listings, sales and sales ratio" jargon on 162 pages; 551 H1s with a "| Surrey Real Estate" tail; 509 titles ending in the brand with 291 over 60 characters; one repeated place name ("White Rock, South Surrey and White Rock").
Done: each of the 1,267 public pages (the 1,264 served by `[...slug].astro` plus the homepage, Fort Langley and Reviews) got a title (38-60), a clean H1 (20-60, no pipe) and a description (135-158, ends in a full stop), written individually from that page's own content. Stored in `src/data/seo-final.json`, built by `tools/build_seo_final.py` from `tools/seo_final/batch_*.json`; applied last in `[...slug].astro`, so deleting an entry falls back to the older layers. The archive records stay untouched. Before/after sheet: `docs/review/seo/seo-before-after-2026-11-05.csv`. Guard: `tests/seo-final.test.mjs`.
Rules held: no month or year figures and no sold statistics (they go stale when the data refreshes); no "Top 1%", "#1", "best"; no phone numbers; brand only where it fits in 60 characters.
Homepage: kept the brand hero; H1 is now "JARED GIBBONS" plus a "South Surrey Realtor" line; description keeps the confirmed Medallion Club claim. Buying and Selling: the H1 is the keyword heading; the old display line ("Buy better", "Sell better") leads the tag line.
Left for Jared / next pass: about 110 pages still show "Draft for Jared's approval" or SnapStats text in their body; ~55 stats-only neighbourhood pages have descriptions built from topics, not landmarks (second pass); downtown-vw and downtown-vancouver-condos-for-sale target the same keyword; new-construction-cloverdale and -cloverdale-bc overlap; brentwood-homes-for-sale body is Brentwood Park; north-delta-lots-land and port-moody-lots-land show the same benchmark price; testimonials H1; homepage body shows archive notes (OI-07, B-07).

## D-146 — SEO pass 2: named specifics in 609 descriptions, 157 title hooks (2026-11-05)
Pass 1 (D-144) left 661 pages whose descriptions named almost nothing real (0 or 1 named places beyond the title). Pass 2 rewrote them from fuller page evidence (local notes, building and complex names, parks, stations, streets, schools, zoning names, neighbouring areas): 661 pages with 0-1 named specifics became 82. Writers were told to use no figures (they go stale), nothing marked unverified or conflicting, and nothing not in the evidence; a script then checked every capitalised name in the new copy against that evidence (two unsupported names, "BCIT" and "GWELLS", were removed by hand). Where the page offered no usable names the earlier text was kept and the page flagged. The 12 market-stats descriptions were left as pass 1: the writers compared each city with arbitrary neighbours, which reads oddly. Files: `tools/seo_final/pass2_*.json` (applied over `batch_*.json` by `tools/build_seo_final.py`); sheet `docs/review/seo/seo-before-after-2026-11-05.csv`.
Page-content conflicts the writers found (the copy avoids them; the pages need Jared's or a research check): Glen Elementary appears on both the Glenwood (Port Coquitlam) and New Horizons (Coquitlam) pages; new-horizons condos and houses pages give conflicting stock; Anmore and Belcarra are their own villages but pages file them under Port Moody; columbia-valley is FVRD Area H, not the City of Chilliwack; cliff-drive is north-west, not central, Tsawwassen; grandview-surrey has a Croydon Drive card with the wrong sub-area and a school name that does not exist; metrotown-burnaby-houses is mostly older low-rise rental and strata, not detached; Ladner Rural guide has no Westham Island section; Brookswood development text says a 6-plex tier that contradicts Bylaw 6020; downtown-ve lists Vancouver West towers; north-coquitlam mixes Burke Mountain and Town Centre; map-search's map does not work for visitors; farms covers all of BC; the two Oaklands pages place it in different spots; sperling-duthie and salmon-river have no condo or townhome stock; the SFU-area page is UniverCity strata, not houses; sullivan-heights take and stock notes disagree; Silver Valley "Horse Hamlet" is verified on one page and unverified on the other; ladner-rural-55-plus has no 55+ stock; several new-construction pages list mostly older stock; 6 "new-listings-this-week" pages and about 80 others have no named places to use.

## D-148 — October stats, RT-A/RT-B, Brookswood, Delta OCP, Mission OCP date, broken sentences (2026-11-06)
October SnapStats (FVR + GVR) are in: the South Surrey presales page "Market snapshot" is now October 2026 (table by home type, sales ratio, days on market). Salmon River (Langley) has no condo or townhome stock, so its condo/townhome page is held (`src/data/local-held/`, skipped by `tools/build_type_pages.py` via `HELD`, every reference removed).
New Westminster: RT-A (s.405) and RT-B (s.406) DO exist (min site area 836.1 m2 / 9,000 sq ft, 20 m frontage, lane access, mainland only). My earlier "they do not exist" correction was wrong; 11 pages restored to the correct wording (Fraserview NW, Brunette, Connaught Heights, Glenbrooke North, Downtown, Lots/Land, Uptown, Queensborough, Sapperton).
Brookswood / Township of Langley Bylaw 6020 (Nov 18, 2024): confirmed tops out at 4 units (3 on lots up to 280 m2, 4 up to 4,050 m2). Eligibility also needs the Urban Containment Boundary, municipal water and sewer, no heritage designation and outside a Transit-Oriented Area; the provincial 6-unit tier near frequent transit is not part of the Township bylaw (City of Langley Bylaw 3284 does allow 6). Pages now say "tops out at 4 units" and add the servicing caveat (some Brookswood lots are still on septic).
Delta OCP: Bylaw No. 8400, 2024 "Housing Our Future", adopted July 8, 2024 (Delta site). Could not verify the old lettered schedule numbers (D.1, B.1, B.3, C.1) or the Bylaw 3950 detail, so all of it was removed from 65 pages; they now state only the verified facts (single consolidated plan, Land Use Plan s.1.5, Agriculture (A), Tsawwassen Urban Centre development permit guidelines, centre designated Neighbourhood Centre and Corridors). Tsawwassen North/Central Schedule D.1 bullets replaced with this.
Mission OCP: City of Mission project page states Bylaw 6405-2026 received third reading and adoption on June 1, 2026 (public hearing April 7, 2026; first and second reading Feb 2 and Mar 2, 2026); it repeals Bylaw 6350-2025. About 20 passages said "April 21, 2026" or cited 6350-2025/5670-2017 as current; all now say June 1, 2026 / 6405-2026.
Grandview Heights Secondary is real (16987 25 Avenue, Surrey, SD36, grades 8-12, opened Sept 2021); no school-name error found on the Grandview pages.
Research-voice cleanup wave 2: 340 passages on 183 pages rewritten or deleted (ALL-CAPS "NOT/NEVER/CONFLICT", "sources differ" rewritten into buyer-facing wording). Broken sentences: a splitter bug in the uniqueness ingest cut sentences at "Bylaw No." / "s." ("...Zoning Bylaw No."); about 200 passages repaired from the source notes (citation completed, research pointers dropped).

## D-149 (6 Nov 2026) Site-wide layout, menu, stats, sources, maps, awards, video
- Awards: homepage strip is now a continuous left-to-right marquee (pauses on hover/focus; static wrap for reduced motion). 18 Royal LePage logos from the award zips (National Top 2% 2022/24/25, Top 5% 2022-25, Top 35 Under 35 2020/23 team/24/25, Executive Circle 2022/24/25, Top Ten Individual BC 2024/25, Award of Excellence 2025 and Lifetime Member) plus the existing Red Diamond, Years of Service and Rookie of the Year images. Run it after any design change; add new page types to its sample list.

## D-158 — Pete's sold export: suites, coach houses and penthouses (2026-11-06)

- **What it is.** Pete's Paragon export (34,752 closed sales, Nov 1, 2025 to Nov 6, 2026, 38 CSVs, no owner names or phone numbers) is kept outside the repo. `python3 -I tools/build_extras_layer.py --dir <folder>` turns it into grouped figures only in `src/data/sold/sold-extras.json` (no MLS numbers, no rows). Same rules as the main sold layer: a median needs 5 sales, a count is mentioned at 3, a building is named at 3 and priced at 5.
- **Window is Nov 1, 2025 to Aug 31, 2026.** The export only has sales Paragon had marked Closed, and later months thin out (803 detached in September, 111 in October, nothing after Oct 9). Everything after August is left out until Pete re-pulls September to today under the other status. `sold-12m.json` and `sold-beds.json` are not touched (they come from the fuller October export), so the held bedroom pages stay held.
- **Fields used.** Suite: `Suite` (Legal Suite or Licensed Suite is "legal"; Unauthorized Suite alone is "unauthorized"). Coach house: `Style of Home` = Carriage/Coach House or Laneway House (the only flag Paragon has; agent-entered, so counts are a floor). Lane access: `Site Influences`. Penthouse: `Style of Home` includes Penthouse on an Apartment/Condo. Kitchens, floor area, lot size, year built, strata fee and bedrooms as in the main layer.
- **Coach house pages (Surrey, Cloverdale, South Surrey)** now carry the flagged-sale counts and prices (9, 24 and 10 sales) with a plain "rough sign, not a trend" caveat where the count is under 15. The old "I can't give you a coach house price" wording is gone.
- **18 new draft pages** (`tools/build_extras_pages.py`): houses with suites for Surrey, Cloverdale, South Surrey, Langley, Abbotsford, Burnaby, Coquitlam, Richmond, Vancouver, North Delta, Maple Ridge and Chilliwack; penthouses for Vancouver, Burnaby, Richmond, Surrey, Coquitlam and New Westminster. The Langley, Abbotsford and South Surrey/White Rock penthouse drafts also gained sold tables. Suite rules are only the sourced lines from the city notes (nothing marked unverified); Mission, Port Coquitlam and Port Moody are left out because their suite rules were not confirmed. Uniqueness is 0.73 to 0.82 on all of them (threshold 0.70). All stay draft; SEO entries are in `tools/seo_final/fix_02.json`; Jerry's worksheet `docs/review/idx/new-searches-2026-11-06.xlsx` now has 29 rows (11 before, 18 new).
- **Not built (under 30 sales):** penthouse pages for Chilliwack (26), Tsawwassen (10), North Delta (4), Ladner (4); coach house pages for any city other than the three above (Vancouver has 42 flagged sales and could get one later).

## D-163 — Pete's September-to-November re-pull merged (2026-11-06)
- **Input:** `sold-export-2026-09-01_2026-11-06.zip` (5,660 sales, 12 CSVs, Closed and Pending; Land and Multifamily files not used by these pages). Kept outside the repo; only grouped figures are stored.
- **Why Pending counts:** Sold Date is the date the sale was made, and a sale only turns Closed weeks later. A Closed-only pull is therefore thin after August (803 detached in September, 111 in October). With Pending added, September and October are complete as of November 6, so the window is now **November 1, 2025 to October 31, 2026** (exactly 12 months, four three-month periods: the last is August to October 2026). November 1–6 is left out so every period is whole.
- **Merge:** `build_extras_layer.py --dir <new> --dir <old> --status Closed,Pending --end 2026-10-31`. Matched on ML #, the new row wins. Pending prices can still change and a few sales may fall through, which the source note on every page now says. August sales that are still Pending (rare) are not in either file.
- **Pages regenerated:** all suite, penthouse, coach house and golf pages (wording moved from "closed sales to August 31" to "closed and pending sales to October 31", "ten months" to "twelve months"). The main sold layer (`sold-12m.json`, to October 22) is a separate pull and is unchanged.

South Surrey keeps its Style of Home search (1451319, 14 listings) because the keyword search covers South Surrey and White Rock together and the page is South Surrey only. Langley stays on 1450209. The sold figures on all of these pages still use Paragon's Style of Home tag.

## D-164: "Email me new listings" signup on every search page (lead machine #1)
- **What:** a compact form (`ListingAlert.astro`) sits directly above the live results on all 1,103 saved-search pages (not on the open map search, my listings or sold widgets). Fields: email (required), first name (optional), an unticked consent box. A site-quality test checks every search page has exactly one, above the results, unticked, with the sender details.
- **Placement:** above the results, not below. The results are a long paginated list; a box under it is seen by the few who reach the end.
- **Delivery:** `POST /api/forms/listing-alert` through the same Worker path as the other forms (Turnstile, honeypot, origin, rate limit). The email to Jared and the Keyvera lead address carries the search name, the MyRealPage search ID, the page URL and the consent record. Reply-to is the visitor.
- **Consent record (CASL):** the Worker, not the browser, adds the exact wording shown (`src/lib/listing-alert.js`, shared by page and Worker) and the timestamp. Without a ticked box the Worker answers 422 and stores nothing. Keyvera contacts made from these emails should get `consentSource` "website new-listing alert box, page + date".
- **Not automatic yet:** nothing sends the alerts by itself. Jared (or a Keyvera campaign) sets up the saved search for each signup; the form says "Jared sets up the alert for you" so the promise matches what happens.

## D-165: "Homes for sale near [school]" pages (wave 1)
- **What:** `tools/build_school_pages.py` builds `homes-near-<school>` pages (draft) for the wave-1 schools in Surrey, South Surrey, White Rock, Cloverdale, Langley, Abbotsford, Delta, Ladner, Tsawwassen and North Delta. Jerry's saved searches (ids 1451413 to 1451582) are wired through `tools/build_idx_embeds.py`; the "Email me new listings" box comes with them (D-164).
- **MyRealPage has no radius option.** Every search matches the full school name in the listing remarks (Jerry, 2026-11-07), so the page says so in plain words: the search finds homes whose description names the school, misses nearby homes that do not, and can include a listing farther than the radius. The only distance-based part is the sold figures (Pete's Paragon radius exports, `tools/build_school_sold.py`, grouped figures only, no rows or addresses stored).
- **Held, not built:** 18 searches with 0 listings (Cedar Hills, Cindrich, David Brankin, East Kensington, Janice Churchill, Port Guichon, Alice Brown, Blacklock Fine Arts, Dorothy Peacock, Langley Fundamental, Wix-Brown, Chief Dan George, King Traditional, Margaret Stenersen, St Bernadette, St John Paul II, Sacred Heart (Ladner), MEI). They are listed in `src/data/schools/wave1-pages.json`. The 6 learning centres were skipped.
- **Content:** school facts only from the BC Data Catalogue and the district sites (`tools/build_school_facts.py`); no ratings, rankings or catchment claims. Catchment is the district's, linked. Sold section only with 10 or more sales within the radius; medians only where 5 or more sold. A page without Pete's figures has no sold section yet and is regenerated when his CSVs arrive.
- **Links:** each page links to its neighbourhood guide, the city schools hub and its three nearest school pages; the hub and the guide link back to every school page. A test requires at least 3 inbound links per page (`tests/school-pages.test.mjs`). Schools with no official coordinates (about 27) use the centre of their postal district for linking only, and no distance is stated for them.

Sentences that only talked about the old page were dropped rather than rewritten, so nothing unverified is now published. Formatting of each file is preserved.

## D-192 — 2026-11-09 — October 2026 board statistics (GVR, FVREB, CADREB)

Jared saved the October packages in Drive ("Board Stats - October 2026"). GVR (released November 2) and FVREB (released November 3) PDFs are kept in `docs/research/market-data/`.
- **Data:** `tools/parse_market.py` now takes `--gvr-date` and `--fvreb-date` and reads the month column header from the PDF text; `src/data/market/2026-10.json` generated from it (September re-parse stays byte-identical). The 22 market-stats pages are rebuilt by `tools/build_market_pages.py` (reads the latest month file).
- **Market-stats pages keep what other tools add.** The generator rebuilds a page from scratch, which wiped the lead-form flag, generic FAQs, the SEO-trimmed title style and the end-of-page link blocks (related links, neighbourhood hubs, launch links, moved notes, the North Vancouver condos link that a test counts). `keep_extras()` now carries these over on every run. Two paragraphs were added so each page stays above 0.70 uniqueness now that its figures are quoted on the Fraser Valley stats page: the year's move in dollars (with six-month, three-year and regional context), and, for the FVREB areas, this year's and last year's sales and active listings with the sales-to-active ratio by type.
- **Draft pages:** the gap-page, new-search, extras and area-gap generators carry the month in their own text. They are not re-run over the live tree (that would undo hand fixes). `tools/refresh_hpi_blocks.py` runs them in a scratch copy and splices in only the blocks that still said September (214 blocks and FAQs on 60 pages), then, with `--numbers`, the generated paragraphs that name no month but quote the benchmark (20 more on 19 pages). Blocks reworded by hand are left alone.
- **SnapStats tables keep September as a column.** The October packages carry no sub-area SnapStats, so community tables show September as their latest month and say so. The methodology page lists the October GVR and FVREB packages and the new CADREB row.
- **CADREB (Chilliwack):** `tools/build_chilliwack_sell_stats.py` adds a dated October section to the Chilliwack sell page from `src/data/market/cadreb-2026-10.json`: sales, new listings, listings at month end, days to sell and sales by price band, for all types and by type. CADREB publishes no benchmark price, so the average sale price is dollar volume divided by sales, labelled as such, and the page says the board's area also covers Agassiz-Harrison, Hope and Cultus Lake. The draft "no price table" FAQ is replaced by two market FAQs. CADREB's October media release is not needed.
- **Fraser Valley stats page** (`src/data/pages/fraser-valley-market-stats.json`, an archive record rebuilt from a widget) moved to October by `tools/refresh_fv_stats_page.py`, which checks each block's September text before replacing it: cards, trend table, by-type and by-area tables, seven area sections and three FAQs. The trend table's earlier months are worked back from the board's published changes and rounded to $1,000; days to sell (detached 50, townhouse 37, apartment 44) come from the board's release. Narrative claims were checked against the October data (rankings, year-on-year sales, listing changes).
- **Left as is on purpose:** the "September 2026 Market Update" blog post (a dated archive post), presale price dates (the date the price was seen), and Google review dates.

## D-193 — 2026-11-09 — External link check, full run (Chrome)

The workspace has no outside network, so every outside link was checked from Chrome: each URL was opened (or requested from inside its own site) and the real status and final address read. `docs/test-results/external-links-2026-11-09.csv` lists all 6,788 unique outside URLs in the build with the domain, page count, example page, result and what was done.
- **Checked:** all 6,788 URLs on about 1,190 sites except the social sites (Facebook, Instagram, LinkedIn, Pinterest, TikTok, YouTube, Vimeo, Google, Yelp, Rate My Agent, MyRealPage), which block automated requests and need a manual look.
- **Cut-off links repaired (68 links in about 50 data files):** the biggest finding. Long PDF links whose filename has spaces had been cut at the first space (FVRD, Pitt Meadows, Port Coquitlam, Abbotsford, Surrey, New West, Chilliwack, TOL and others), so they pointed at a file that never existed. The full filenames were recovered from the research notes, encoded, and each one opened to confirm it is live.
- **Moved pages repointed (about 140 links):** Surrey (OCP, West Newton plan, bylaws, secondary suites, small-scale housing), TOL (bylaws, plans, Langley Events Centre), Abbotsford (zoning, OCP), Burnaby (tax rates), Maple Ridge, Port Moody, New West (parks), Delta (flood, recreation centres, `admin.delta.ca` back-end host swapped to `www.delta.ca`), Mission, North Vancouver school sites (now one site per school), gov.bc.ca (strata finances, depreciation reports, wells), CRA GST/HST new housing rebate, CMHC, Richmond library, Rennie Silva.
- **Clean-up:** 97 tracking tags removed from outbound links (`utm_source=chatgpt.com`, `utm_*`, `fbclid`, `gclid`, `_hsenc`, a BC Assessment session token), `staging.mikestewart.ca` and `staging.newwestcity.ca` swapped to the live hosts, a link with a stray `):` fixed.
- **Links to pages that are gone for good (92 URLs, 143 links):** removed; the words stay, the link comes off. One standalone link card (Save-On-Foods map) and one empty links block were removed with them. These are mostly old council documents, expired developer sites and closed pages. List: the "replaced or link removed" rows in the CSV.
- **Kept on purpose:** 403 and 429 results (bot walls and rate limits, the pages exist), redirects that still land on a working page, and links that moved hosts but still work (`illustrator.uservoice.com`, `stgeorgesvillage.ca`).
- **Still to do:** the social links; and a second full run after launch so the live listing and IDX pages are included. Until then the listing links are not covered.

## D-194 — 2026-11-09 — Copy QA from the Cursor verification review

A second review (Cursor, checked against the launch build `PUBLIC_PREVIEW=false`) confirmed a set of copy and template problems. Every confirmed item was checked again here before changing anything; the ones marked "left" below were judged not to be mistakes.
- **Research notes on public pages:** the notes builders carried the researchers' voice onto ~920 pages ("not verified in the sources read", "in any pass", "Fresh-verified", "What is not confirmed about X" sections, label-less lines starting with ": "). `tools/clean_research_voice.py` (idempotent) drops those sentences, keeps the factual part of a sentence before a research clause, removes the "What is not confirmed" sections and the headings left empty, trims research asides from building headings, and cleans table source lines. `tools/build_uniqueness.py` no longer publishes its NOT CONFIRMED list. Result in the launch HTML: "not confirmed" 2,386 lines to 2 (both harmless), "not verified" 1,755 to 0.
- **Uniqueness after the clean-up:** 6 guides and 4 other pages now sit just under the 0.70 unique-text bar (lowest 0.675). The removed text was not useful to a reader, so this is accepted; those pages are first in line for new local copy.
- **Blog:** the RSS link above every post title (200 posts) and on the blog index pointed at a feed the site serves as 410; removed.
- **Listings:** the "listing feed is not connected in the preview" sentence on active listings is now preview-only. 6469 Meadow Place's past open houses (Oct 31, Nov 1) and the "OPEN HOUSE Sat/Sun 1-3" line are removed. 13 addresses listed twice now carry the MLS® number in the H1. No space before punctuation in the vendor stats line.
- **Awards block on 41 pages:** "4x Medallion Club Qualifier", "Top 10% in the FVREB" and "Royal LePage Diamond Award (2025)" (Diamond was 2016–2020) replaced with the site's current lines: Top 1% of all Realtors in the Fraser Valley; 10 straight years in the FVREB Medallion Club; Royal LePage National Top 2% (2022, 2024, 2025); Top 10 Royal LePage individual in BC (2024, 2025).
- **Jump links:** 613 links to section ids that do not exist (old `#jg-overview`-style menus and `/#…` links that sent readers to the homepage) removed; menus left empty removed; links to another page keep the page and lose the dead fragment. The presale page's "Request floorplans" cards (`#vip-floorplan-modal`, no such modal) now go to `/contact.html`.
- **Generated wording:** "a sales ratio of —." and "of not published for the month" removed or rewritten (generator fixed in `build_snapstats_layers.py`); dangling "—." fixed; "Between November 2025 to" now "From November 2025 to" (`build_gvr_pages.py`); "real estate real estate" fixed in `build_explore.py` and `explore.json` regenerated (only the place names changed); "Guide Guide view" fixed in the related-card template; 51 headings with a doubled word fixed ("Rural homes Homes", "Belmont Elementary Elementary"); "solds" now "sales"; empty headings removed; a stray "Free property check" line removed from 122 guide files (the form itself is unchanged).
- **Source labels:** repeat numbers ("Chilliwack.com (2)") no longer shown; 1,250 cut-off labels completed from the link address; publishers' own misspellings corrected in displayed titles.
- **Reviews:** reviewer first names on the reviews, buying and selling pages are now initials, like the rest of the site.
- **Spelling:** the listed typos fixed (Cambridge, Scottsdale, Alouette, Pattullo, Burnett Street, Assignment, Trethewey, Tsawwassen, Oakridge, Millennium, townhouses, an elevator, hot tub and others); Canadian spelling in copy (neighbourhood 185, centre 67, favourite 9, neighbour 31, metres). Links are not changed.
- **Design:** home hero name back to weight 800; off-palette colours moved to tokens (calculator errors in ink, not red; breadcrumb greys; cream fallback). jared.html's H2 that repeated the H1, and its "About Form" heading, renamed. An H2 that repeats the page H1 is now dropped by the template.
- **Left on purpose:** Cabe Secondary (a real school); the White Rock apostrophe; "Simon Fraser Univer." (the board's own area name); typos inside quoted Google reviews (verbatim); the -ise/-ize mix (style only); excerpt dates the review export does not have (no dates are made up); the "Top South Surrey Realtor" post headline (Jared's stated basis: #1 South Surrey agent by FVREB Medallion Club points).
- **Guard:** `tests/copy-hygiene.test.mjs` fails the build's test run if any of these patterns come back.

## D-196 — 2026-11-09 — Local copy for the pages under the 0.70 unique-text bar

After the research-wording clean-ups (D-194) 11 pages sat at 0.68–0.70. The cause was shared text: sub-areas carry their city's rules (Surrey suites and SSMUH, Maple Ridge, Abbotsford ALR, FVRD Area D for both Popkum and Bridal Falls) word for word. A second wording pass also removed "the feed", "the page is headed", "none verified" and similar from ~500 pages.
- `tools/build_local_story.py` (gen 'story', idempotent) adds copy only that page carries:
  - **History and landmarks, from primary sources:** Bridal Falls (post office of 1937 and why it is not "Bridal Veil Falls", the 60 m falls and winter ice warning, park facilities; BC Geographical Names, BC Parks, FVRD), Popkum (official community, Popkum First Nation reserves, the "In the Shadow of Mt. Cheam" history, FVRD services and the quarterly crime summary), Sumas Prairie (Sumas Lake, the Sinclair Plan, Barrowtown pumps of 1923, the 1926 hop yards; Heritage Abbotsford), Burnaby Hospital (the pavilion that opened March 10, 2026; Fraser Health), College Park (the Barnet Highway's history).
  - **Sales story from Jared's Paragon export** on the guides (middle half of prices, typical bedrooms, build year, lot or strata fee, days on market, sale-to-list, price per sq ft, the price by quarter) and, on type pages that already show the sold table, only the price by quarter. Same rules as the sold layer: a median needs 5 sales.
  - **Copied paragraphs** on six pages (GST and PTT buyer rules, Form B, suites, floodplain, park and commute notes) now point to the one page that carries the full text, so a reader is one click from it and the copy is not repeated.
- Result: every guide and new page is at 0.70 or above (guides under the bar 0, new pages under 0.70: 0). Build clean, 151 tests pass.

## D-197 — 2026-11-09 — Seven UX changes from the site review (Jared approved all seven)

1. **Search pages:** the "Email me new listings" box is now a one-line bar above the results that opens into the form on tap (open by default on wide screens). It stays above the results (D-129's reason holds: a box under a long paginated list is rarely seen), but phones now see homes on the first screen.
2. **Quick facts on guides:** a box under the H1 with the median sale price by home type and what sold (Nov 2025 to Oct 2026, Paragon), plus jump buttons (Homes for sale, Schools, Parks, Before you buy, Questions). Built by `tools/build_guide_facts.py` into `src/data/guide-facts.json` (109 guides, 102 with prices); a guide that matches no single MLS® sub-area gets jump buttons only, never a guessed price.
3. **Folded reference sections:** Sources and the disclaimer on every page, and on guides the rules, costs, risks, development, building-by-building, comparison, related and what's-nearby sections, open with a tap. The H2 stays inside `<summary>` and the text stays in the HTML; a link to a section inside a fold opens it. West Newton on a phone: 51 screens to 30.
4. **Tap targets:** links that stand alone in lists, fact rows and one-link paragraphs are at least 24px tall (WCAG 2.2 2.5.8); links inside running text are exempt.
5. **Home hero proof line:** "★ 5.0 · 95 Google reviews · Top 1% Fraser Valley" (Jared's wording; the reviews part links to /reviews.html).
6. **Home buttons:** "Search homes", "Call 604-928-1361", "Email Jared".
7. **Phones:** the black phone/email strip is hidden under 40rem; the sticky bottom bar already has Call.

## D-199 — 2026-11-10 — Two-day review (Nov 8–10): what was wrong and what changed

Full review of the work since Nov 8: launch and preview builds, every internal link through the real Worker router (640,190 links, 0 broken; listing photos are served from R2), every image source, all 1,892 pages at phone width, JSON-LD, titles and descriptions, and a copy scan of the public text.

**Bugs I introduced and fixed**
- **D-197 tap-target rule broke the source cards on every page.** `main li > a:not(.btn)…{display:inline-block}` also hit `.src`, `.chip` and `.dp-card`, so source cards lost their card layout and, on 11 Delta pages, a long host name pushed the page 67px wider than a phone. The rule now applies to classless links only; source cards also get `minmax(0,1fr)` so a long host truncates. Phone overflow: 11 pages to 0.
- **"Sub-area medians are not published"** (20 pages) contradicted the new quick-facts prices; it meant the board. Now "The board does not publish sub-area medians".
- **D-194 research-wording passes missed "verified"** as a research note (650 uses on 329 public pages: "no verified condo stock", "None was verified", "How many verified … on this roster"). `tools/clean_copy_pass4.py` rewrites those to "known" / "that I know of" / "is known"; buyer instructions ("must be verified in the depreciation report", "verify it on title") stay. Public uses of "verified" 650 to under 200, the rest instructions.
- **Langley City's SkyTrain date** had dropped out of Jared's take while tidying; restored ("expected to open in late 2029").

**Older errors found and fixed**
- 51 sentences where an old generator dropped the area name ("rental/pet lines in are listing data") now read "here".
- Internal jargon on public pages: "banked in the L3 Richmond batch", "stated for edge completeness", "this scope", "counted entries", "on this roster", "index listings".
- Doubled words ("specific specific", "records records", "parcel parcel-by-parcel"), ". ." endings, a capital "Published" mid-sentence (10), "the export" (now "the MLS® data").
- Source-card titles built from file names ("News122 Excise GST HST News No 122", "OCP 2024 web", "Node", "96448 01", "R748"): a URL title map and rules in `src/lib/sources.ts`; poor titles 1,169 cards to 51.
- The VIP floorplans form had no privacy link; every FormStub form now shows one when its fine print doesn't mention privacy.

**Checked and fine:** launch build has no preview notes, drafts or archive notes; the 6 noindex pages are deliberate holds; 95-review count consistent on all 310 pages; VIP card prefill and focus work; folded sections open from a hash or jump link; West Newton sales story matches the sold data; Fort Langley, Aldergrove, Salmon River and Willoughby figures in Jared's take match the sold data.

**Tests:** copy-hygiene now also scans `src/data/designed` and blocks research "verified", the medians line, dropped area names and generator doubles.

## D-200 — 2026-11-10 — Second review: the D-193 repointed links, October figures, launch switch

**Repointed outside links (D-193):** all 93 outside URLs that run added (290 links: repoints, cut-off repairs, host swaps) were opened again in Chrome. 91 loaded; the two Safeway store pages had moved again (404) and now go to `safeway.ca/stores/safeway-austin-heights` and `/stores/safeway-mcbride`. Fourteen repoints had gone to a parent page although the exact page exists: Abbotsford zoning bylaw and OCP (now the City's bylaw library), Maple Ridge zoning bylaw, Port Moody zoning and OCP, New Westminster zoning bylaw, Ladner Leisure Centre, Delta flood protection (the drainage page that holds it), Surrey Fleetwood Town Centre Plan, and a road-closures link that pointed at page 2. Labels that read as file names were fixed, the Seasons Wesbrook name updated to Chartwell Wesbrook Village (the operator's own page), and the CMHC First-Time Home Buyer Incentive link marked "closed to new applications". `tools/fix_repointed_links_2026_11_10.py`; every new target opened and checked.

**Links to other agents' sites:** about 690 source links point at other agents' and brokerages' sites (bccondosandhomes.com 426 page-links, mikestewart.ca 113, homehappy.ca 62, danmarusin.com 35 and others). They stay as citations but now carry `rel="nofollow"` (post-build, `src/integrations/fix-links.mjs`), so the site passes no ranking credit to competitors. Over time these are worth replacing with developer or City sources.

**October board figures:** `src/data/market/2026-10.json` was checked against both October PDFs line by line: all 125 benchmark rows (benchmark, index, one-year change) and all 547 FVREB summary numbers match. The site was then searched for benchmarks still tied to September: 9 hand-written archive pages had them. The South Surrey & White Rock snapshot tables on Morgan Creek, Crescent Beach & Ocean Park and Grandview Heights (Morgan Creek's heading already said October over September figures) are now October (released November 3, 2026); the Maple Ridge and Pitt Meadows house and townhome pages now quote the October GVR benchmark (their Maple Ridge figures, $746,600 and $1,231,900, had not been September's either); North Vancouver condos had October's figure labelled September. `tools/refresh_september_leftovers.py`. The September market-update post and the SnapStats sub-area tables (no October SnapStats) stay September on purpose.

**Launch blocker fixed:** `robots.txt` opened only with a separate `PUBLIC_LAUNCH=1` flag that the launch checklist never sets, so a launch build would have shipped `Disallow: /` for the whole site. It now follows `PUBLIC_PREVIEW=false` like every page's meta robots. Tested.

**Technical pass (launch build, 1,489 pages):** titles 30–65 characters, descriptions 120–159, no duplicates; every page has a canonical to itself (404 excepted); sitemap 1,886 URLs (preview) with no noindex page in it and every indexable page in it; FAQ schema questions all visible on the page; heaviest page 336 KB.

## D-201 — 2026-11-10 — "Updated <month>" and byline on guides

Every guide shows "Updated November 2026 · By Jared Gibbons, REALTOR®" under the H1 (`GenericPage.astro`). The month is the month after the newest `src/data/market/YYYY-MM.json`, i.e. when that board package came out, so it moves with each monthly refresh; nothing to edit by hand. Guides also emit `WebPage` JSON-LD with `dateModified` (the page's own content-change date from `src/data/lastmod.json`) and the agent as author.

Now each type section keeps its lead, its building/complex entries and its local notes once ("Where it sits and what to know here"); city-wide rules paragraphs move to one page per city; BC-wide rules (Form B, ALR, transfer tax, GST, REDMA, wells) are not copied there because the guides already link to the BC rules pages; type-section sources merge into one list; a type written under two spellings of the area (Aldergrove / Aldergrove Langley; Cultus Lake East / North / South) is merged under one heading. Two question sections become one at render time: the local questions join the archive's own section, each question once, so archive answers keep their links.

## D-204 — 2026-11-10 — Sources on other agents' sites replaced

The 1,050 source links that pointed at other agents' and listing portals' pages (599 addresses) are swapped when the page is built, from `src/data/source-replacements.json` (`tools/build_source_replacements.py`, applied in `GenericPage.astro` to every list under a Sources heading), so every data layer and any regenerated page is covered:

Those records are not public pages, so there is nothing better to link. The line is kept under eight words so that, repeated on 300 pages, it adds no shared wording to the uniqueness check.

## D-205 — 2026-11-10 — Guide format v2, piloted on Murrayville

From the review of a competing agent's Murrayville post. Driven by `src/data/guide-plus.json` (one entry per guide, `status: draft` until Jared approves), rendered by `GenericPage.astro`:
- **Quick answer** first under the H1: what the area is, the median sale prices by type (from `sold-12m.json` at build time) and Jared's one-line verdict.
- **Quick facts** gain "Typical house sold" (year built, lot, bedrooms), "Drive, off-peak" (Highway 1, Surrey Central SkyTrain, downtown Vancouver, measured on OpenStreetMap routing with no traffic, so labelled off-peak with "rush hour takes longer") and "What it feels like".
- **What works and what doesn't** near the top: Jared's own points marked "Jared"; the rest are sourced facts already on the page. No superlatives without a source.
- **Is X right for you?** before the questions: 3–4 questions with straight answers and links to better-fit areas, then "Book a call with Jared" (calendly.com/jaredgibbons, checked) and the phone number.
- **Video slot:** a YouTube id in the entry shows a tap-to-play tour (no YouTube code loads until tapped); nothing shows without one.
- Two search-style questions per guide ("Is X a good place to live?", "What are the downsides of living in X?") lead the page's questions and its FAQ schema.
Our prices are closed-sale medians with the window stated, not board benchmarks carried from another month. Murrayville is the pilot; the verdicts and trade-offs for the other guides come from Jared.

## D-206 — 2026-11-10 — Milner guide (from Jared's content pack)

`/milner-langley-guide.html`, draft, in the v2 format (`tools/build_milner_guide_2026_11_10.py`). Changed from the pack: no SnapStats figures (SnapStats and Paragon have no Milner area, and a Langley-wide median is not a Milner price, so the page says so and offers the sold list); sources moved to primary ones (TransLink stop list instead of a fan wiki, BC Gov News, Langley Retired Teachers' Association, Canada's Historic Places, the ALC's housing page; our own zoning page and a contractor's site are not used as sources); bus frequency dropped (the pack's figure didn't match the trip planner) and the 562 described only as Glover Road to Langley Centre and Carvolth Exchange; ALR housing corrected (one additional residence up to 90 m² is now allowed without an application, if Township zoning allows); "truck route" and the 2004 closing year not stated. ## D-223 — 2026-11-11 — Fluff pass
Jared asked to remove fluff and unnecessary words. `tools/trim_fluff_2026_11_11.py` (archive pages, local layer, blog posts; never reviews, quotes, Jared's take, works/doesn't lists or listing remarks) cut about 47,000 words across 740 files:
- method notes repeated on up to 336 pages ("Facts here come from…", "Walking and driving times were measured…", "For each building named below…", "Facts that neighbouring buildings share…", "Details change…", "Facts come from the official sources…", "What the official sources say about the homes and lots…", "This page names each building…") cut or cut to one short line; sources lists stay.
- "The BC rules that apply to every X purchase are explained once, with sources, on the page linked below." plus its one-link list (1,116 places) is now one line: "The province-wide rules are on one page: BC X buying rules."
- research shorthand in plain words: statute citations dropped from the ALR line (sources list them), the ALR residence caps and the farm-class thresholds written as sentences, "ALR rules (for completeness — …)" and "ALR block (provincial frame…)" become "If a parcel is in the ALR:".
- filler: "in order to", "It's important to note that", "In today's market,", "Additionally/Furthermore/Moreover,", "truly", "nestled in", "boasts/boasting", "a wide range of", "stunning/vibrant/sought-after" before a noun, and "Whether you're X or Y," openers (kept where the clause names places, since it carries the page's search terms).
Thin pages: only Contact (213 words) is under 300 words among indexable pages; the newsletter and leave-a-review forms are noindex (D-221). `tests/data-rules.test.mjs` accepts the rules link inline.

## D-229 — 2026-11-11 — Jared's line for each price-range and bedroom page (drafts sent)
`tools/draft_price_page_takes_2026_11_11.py` drafts one line per page (85: 61 price-range, 24 bedroom) from the 12-month sales layers: for a price page, the share of the city's sales in that page's own slice (sibling pages get different slices) and the neighbourhoods where that slice buys a typical home, plus a practical check that fits the stock; for a bedroom page, the best-value and dearest neighbourhood and the busiest building. No two drafts are the same. Sent to Jared as the Drive sheet "For Jared: one line for each price and bedroom page" (docs/review/price-page-takes-for-jared-2026-11-11.csv). Nothing is published in his voice until he approves or rewrites each line; the approved lines will render as "Jared's take" on each page.
D-229, round 2 (same evening): Jared's review of all 85 drafts (docs/review/price-page-takes-review-2026-11-11.xlsx: 10 right, 75 with a suggested line) found real errors in the first drafts: "under $X" pages quoted one price slice instead of the share of all sales under $X (Abbotsford condos under $800K: 15% in the draft, about 99% in fact); "thin slice" on ranges holding a third of sales; city acreage counts on the over-$1.8M pages that don't belong to that price range; one build year averaged across areas 20+ years apart; White Rock called part of South Surrey; a 55+ leasehold complex named as a 2-bedroom townhome top seller. `tools/price_page_takes_v2_2026_11_11.py` adopts the review's lines, adds back the bedroom pages' area medians and busiest buildings from sold-beds.json with the data shown in the sheet (ties named as ties; retirement and 55+ complexes and their namesakes excluded), and marks the 5 pages that need Jared's own words. Sheet "For Jared: price and bedroom page lines, round 2 (approve)" replaces round 1 (trashed). The round-1 generator stays as the record of what was wrong.
D-229, round 2b: the second review flagged 7 bedroom rows (a building split across two areas counted twice, area lists covering under 80% of sales, a repeated sentence, "close" instead of naming the cheapest and dearest area, a top-building claim the data could not settle, the MLS label "Cloverdale BC"). Bedroom lines are now rebuilt from the data: area claims only at 80%+ coverage, cheapest and dearest always named ("slightly" when within 5%), the top building from the city-wide building list with 5+ sales (ties named), and the data column shows every area, the smaller areas' total and every building with 3+ sales. Cottonwood Village (Chilliwack) stays out pending Jared's answer on whether it is part of the 55+ complex. The sheet was replaced (same title).

## D-230 — 2026-11-11 — Whole-site polish, every page
`tools/polish_scan.py` reads the main content of every built page (1,905) for template leftovers, spacing and doubled punctuation, "1 bedrooms", "a 18%", stray markdown, lowercase after a full stop, empty and duplicate headings and 60+-word sentences. Fixed from it:
- Listing pages: the archive's second "Area Statistics" heading (and any heading with nothing after it) is dropped in ListingPage; property cards say "1 bedroom / 1 bathroom" to screen readers; three sold listings lose old "**Open House…**" / "**Subject Free…**" notices.
- "sold at a 18% sales ratio" → "sold at a sales ratio of 18%" (54 local files and the vary_templates variant).
- `tools/polish_headings_2026_11_11.py`: "Where it sits and what to know here" (under every property-type section on 83 guides) is named for the type; where "Good to know about X" / "Streets, schools and getting around X" repeat on a page they name the type (207 files).
- `tools/polish_local_places_2026_11_11.py`: section headings carried the page title as the place ("Good to know about Willoughby Guide", "Townhome complexes in Yaletown Condos for Sale Townhomes"); 62 such names are now the plain place. "Queue" and "…at the rule level only" research sections are removed.
- `tools/sweep_research_voice_pass5_2026_11_11.py`: bookkeeping lines ("Cross-reference only", "sub-area rows sum exactly to the community totals in both splits", "(secondary source)", "listing-level data", "A listing aggregator records") removed or plain-worded, no fact changed (139 strings, 77 files).
- Other agents' sites in source lists: D-204 already swaps them at render time (developer or government page, or the "Building facts" line). A first pass here dropped 1,055 such items from the data, which also lost those swaps; it was undone the same evening (all 439 lists restored). What changed instead: the 55+ pages' list after "Facts on this page come from these public sources…" (no Sources heading) now goes through the same swap and renders as source cards, so murphyrealestategroup.ca and similar no longer show there; 33 more agent and portal domains joined src/data/other-agent-domains.json. `tools/polish_source_links_2026_11_11.py` is kept for reference; do not re-run it.
- Rich.astro: link text still in the "host · page slug" form reads "Publisher: Title" through lib/sources.ts (e.g. "Township of Langley: About the Township", "TransLink: Route 503 timetable (PDF)").
- Empty headings hidden: bradner "Schools Serving Bradner (SD34)", chilliwack acreages/duplexes "Floodplain Notes", Maple Ridge townhome "Local rules" (no rules under it).
Left as is: 315 long sentences (archive lists of schools, zoning terms; splitting them by script would garble them), duplicate sub-labels inside cards ("Ask / Look at / Watch for"), "an 11-storey" style a/an hits (correct), lowercase starts in client reviews (their words).
D-230, accessibility and layout: axe (WCAG 2.0–2.2 A/AA + best practice), sideways scroll and console errors on all 1,905 pages at 1440 px and 390 px (3,810 page-views): no page scrolls sideways, no load errors. The one axe finding (landmark-unique, 2 pages) is fixed: table regions without a caption take the heading above them as their name, and chip link bars add that heading to their label. Console errors left are the MyRealPage search (blocked in the test sandbox) and listing photos (served from R2 by the Worker, not in the static build).

## D-237 — 2026-11-12 — Instagram on the home page (built, waiting on the feed)
"Follow along @jaredgibbonsrealestate": the six latest posts in a square grid (6 across on desktop, 3 on phones), each opening the post on Instagram, plus a "Follow on Instagram" button; before the closing "Ready to get started?" band. Source: a **Behold** JSON feed (behold.so, free: 6 posts, daily refresh, 1,200 requests/month). The Worker (`src/worker/instagram.js`, `/api/instagram.json`) asks Behold at most once an hour and serves its cached copy, so visitors never use up the free allowance and no third-party script loads on the page. The section stays hidden until posts arrive. To switch on: Jared connects @jaredgibbonsrealestate at behold.so and creates a JSON feed; its URL (`https://feeds.behold.so/<id>`, public) goes in `wrangler.jsonc` vars as `INSTAGRAM_FEED_URL`.

## D-240 — 2026-11-12 — Speed
- **Instant next page:** Astro prefetch (`prefetchAll`, hover strategy): a link's page starts loading on hover (desktop) or touch (phones).
- **Browser caching** (`cacheFor()` in route.js): `/_astro/` (content-hashed) and `/fonts/` one year immutable; images a week (+1 day stale-while-revalidate); search index an hour; pages still revalidate every time so edits show at once.
- **Measured** (launch build, phone 390 px at 2×, slow 4G 1.6 Mb/s + 150 ms, CPU slowed 4×, local server without compression, third parties blocked): LCP home 1.84 s, area page 1.36 s, guide 1.40 s, mobile homes 1.36 s, listing 0.99 s, blog post 1.19 s, map hub 1.38 s; CLS 0–0.033. Google's "good" is LCP under 2.5 s and CLS under 0.1. On Cloudflare (Brotli, HTTP/3, edge) it will be faster.
- At launch, once the zone is active: turn on Early Hints and 0-RTT in Cloudflare Speed settings. Not Rocket Loader (it rewrites scripts and breaks the forms and maps).
GoDaddy records (Jared's screenshot, 9): the 6 real records match the Cloudflare import; the other 3 are GoDaddy's NS ×2 and SOA, which Cloudflare replaces with its own.

Corrections to D-246: `hope.ca/p/tax-rates` is a 404 even on https (the earlier check was wrong), now `https://hope.ca/tax-rates`; the cut-off FVRD link on the two Hemlock pages now goes to the full "FVRD Evacuation Guide - Hemlock Valley" PDF (answers 200). New: BC Assessment "Apply for farm classification" links lose their tracking query string (5 links; the plain address loads); two more New Westminster addresses outside `src/data/local` repointed (Sapperton construction projects, 2024 press releases). RFP 26012 is no longer listed by FVRD, so that link stays on the FVRD tenders page.
- Breadcrumbs: listing pages' last crumb is now the listing's own address in the schema (was `/our-listings.html`). 84 area, complex and guide pages that sat directly under Home now sit under their city (`AREA_PARENT` in `tools/build_breadcrumbs.py`), e.g. Home > South Surrey > Morgan Crossing. Pages that set their own two-step trail (market stats, schools, checklists, tools) are unchanged. The build tools no longer write `/index.html` for Home.
- Not changed: listing photo alt text ("Photo N: address", 3,539 photos). The archive has photo descriptions for 29 listings only, and their counts do not match the photo counts, so they cannot be matched to photos safely. Describing each photo needs a separate pass that looks at every picture. Offered to Jared.

## D-248 — 2026-11-12 — Broken "index" sections and split text, site-wide (Jared: "go through them all")
- MyRealPage contact phone set to 604-928-1361 (Listings > Settings > Photo & Contact), approved by Jared; the listing frames now show it.
- Numbered index sections ("Langley Sub-Areas", "Farm Markets", the Brookswood resources, a broken "Explore More") were printing as loose lines: number, name, tags, arrow. They now render as one card grid (`GenericPage.astro`, the 'related' block with a label line and its own link text). Numbered steps ("01" / title / text) render as step cards (new 'steps' block). Bare section numbers above main headings are dropped.
- New `mend()` in `src/lib/upgrade.ts`, run on every archive page before layout: rejoins sentences the export split at a link or bold phrase ("The" / "White Rock Pier is…", "(" / link / ")"), puts "· link" rows back on one line or into a link list, and drops decoration kept as text (lone "+", "-", arrows, emoji). No wording is changed.
- Two-word labels ending in a preposition ("Watch for") now count as labels, so the "Look at / Ask / Watch for" lists on the guides render as cards. A short label above a heading or card group is set as a kicker; the same label stranded at the end of a section is dropped.
- Communities page: the nine "View Listings" lines with no link are now linked cards.
- Measured on the built site (fragments in body text, before → after): single connective words 135 → 24, lines starting with punctuation 342 → 36, lines ending mid-sentence 607 → 177, link labels with no link 42 → 4. Word-level comparison of every page before and after: nothing lost except the decoration and stranded labels above.
- Still open (long tail, not yet fixed): about 130 pages with a smaller glitch of this kind, mostly guide cards where one label sits outside its card, the stat row on the landlord-tenant page, and blog posts with "-" lead-ins.

## D-249 — 2026-11-12 — Search titles and snippets for all 203 listing pages (Jared: "the seo and meta for all our sold listings")
- Title: address, city, what it is and its status, then the name, within 60 characters: "17392 2 Avenue, Surrey BC – Sold House | Jared Gibbons". Was "17392 2 Avenue, Surrey, BC – MLS R2490508". The name is dropped first when the address is long. An address listed twice carries its MLS® number so no two titles match. Six hand-written archive titles ("Acreage Home Sold in Otter District – Langley Realtor") are kept; stale ones ("For Sale:" on a sold home) are rebuilt.
- Description: one factual line for every listing, within 158 characters: status, month and price, address with its MLS® sub-area, beds, baths, type, size, year, and Jared's name. Active listings end with the phone number. 64 listings used to show a cut-off piece of MLS marketing text with no address; that text stays in the page body.
- No claim that Jared was the listing agent: some of these were listed by other brokerages (the credit line says so).
- H1 unchanged (address, city). Checked on the build: 203 titles, none over 60, none repeated; 203 descriptions, none over 158, none repeated.

## D-250 — 2026-11-12 — A real description for every listing photo (Jared: "yes do the listing photo descriptions")
- All 3,338 gallery photos (3,539 placements on 203 listing pages) now have alt text that says what the photo shows ("Covered deck with stone fireplace and sectional", "Aerial view of the acreage with the boundary outlined"), written by looking at each photo on numbered contact sheets. Stored by photo id in `src/data/photo-alts.json` (`tools/merge_photo_alts.py` builds it). The first photo of each listing also names the address. The old "Photo 3: address | MLS" is only a fallback now; none are left on the build.

## D-251 — 2026-11-12 — Listing descriptions rewritten per home (Jared: "address, MLS and the postal ... and sold", "within the characters")
- Every listing's meta description now leads with the status and full address, postal code where known, and MLS® number, then the home (beds, type, MLS® sub-area), what stands out in its own photos (pool, suite, detached shop, carriage house, rooftop deck, views, barn, hot tub…, from `src/lib/listing-features.ts` reading `photo-alts.json`), size or year built when room is left, then the sale month and price. Example: "Sold: 23049 134 Loop, Maple Ridge BC V4R 0E6, MLS® R2782794. House in Silver Valley with a pool, a suite and a double garage. Sold Jun 2023 for $1,510,000."
- Length: every one is 155 characters or less (checked on the build: 90 to 155, average 134), none repeated. Parts drop from the end (third feature, size, sub-area, beds) until it fits; the address, postal code, MLS® number and price are kept.
- Postal codes: 95 of 203 came from the archive; the other 108 were looked up address by address on Google Maps in Jared's Chrome (OpenStreetMap was tried first and disagreed with Google on every one checked, so it was not used). All 203 now carry a postal code (`src/data/listing-postal.json`). Checked on the build: 203 of 203 with postal code and MLS® number, 98 to 155 characters.

## D-252 — 2026-11-12 — Full-site audit (rounds 1–4), each item re-checked on the current build
- Already fixed earlier today and confirmed: phone action bar (D-245; re-measured on 51 of the flagged pages at 375, 390 and 430: nothing on the first screen is covered, and nothing at the end of a page); `/index.html` links and redirect; listing breadcrumb schema; photo alt text; dead city links; "needs.Use", "the The"; sitemap blog URLs (no leading hyphen in the sitemap); MyRealPage phone; `INDEXABLE` is "true" in `wrangler.jsonc`.
- Buttons: `.btn`, `.chip` and the quick-fact links are now at least 44px tall (`2.75rem`). A tap-size scan of 10 page types at 390px finds nothing under 44px except checkboxes and radios, whose labels are the larger tap area.
- Back-to-top link is inside a `nav` landmark; the Fort Langley tools box is a section, not a nested `aside`. axe-core on 20 pages (including the 3 the audit could not scan): no violations.
- Copy: review quote "jared.Selling" / "condo.Hope" spaced (review excerpts and the selling page); four more run-together sentences found by a site-wide scan (one listing, Hatzic, Dewdney-Deroche).
- `/api/instagram.json` and `/api/reviews.json` answer 204 (empty) until a feed is connected, instead of 404, so pages log no error; the pages only read a 200.
- Selling page: the two 1600px photos now offer the existing 800px copies to phones. Home page portrait has a 480px copy.
- Not changed, with reasons: Google Maps "OverQuota" (138 pages) is the Maps key's daily cap in Google Cloud, hit by the audit's 1,900-page crawl; the area map loads normally on a fresh visit (checked live). MyRealPage's `embeddable.less` error is the vendor script asking for its own stylesheet across domains; the listing grid sits in MyRealPage's frame and renders with the brand styling (checked live), so it is console noise only. Mobile LCP: with the site's own files only (third-party blocked, 4x CPU, ~1.6 Mbps) LCP is 1.4–1.8 s on the four slow pages the audit named; maps and the listing grid already load only near the screen; the rest of the gap is Google Analytics/Ads, Maps and MyRealPage scripts.

## D-253 — 2026-11-12 — Google reviews on /reviews.html from Google Places until the Business Profile feed is approved (Jared: "Google reviews feed: still not connected")
- The Business Profile API feed (workers/reviews, BL-03) still waits on Google's access approval and OAuth secrets. Until it answers, the page falls back to Google Places in the visitor's browser with the existing restricted Maps key: average rating, review count and up to 5 recent reviews (the most Places returns), place ID ChIJi3WbELfEhVQRCk86YYxb5us.
- Shown unedited, attributed to Google, author name linked to their Google profile, "See all reviews on Google" and "Leave a review" links. No dates (Jared asked to drop them on review labels). No star-rating structured data (D-19).
- Cost control: requested only when the visitor scrolls near the reviews, and kept 12 hours in that browser. The reviews field is a paid Places field (Place Details, Enterprise + Atmosphere); at this page's traffic it stays within or near Google's monthly free allowance.
- When the Business Profile feed has data, it wins and Places is not called.

## D-254 — 2026-11-12 — Instagram feed connected (Jared: "can you do Instagram feed"; he signed in to Behold and approved the Instagram connection himself)
- Behold free plan, source @jaredgibbonsrealestate (Basic), one JSON feed "jaredgibbons.ca homepage". Its address is public and set as INSTAGRAM_FEED_URL in wrangler.jsonc, so it deploys with the code.
- The Worker asks Behold at most once an hour and serves its own copy, so visitors do not count against Behold's 1,200 monthly views.
- Photo descriptions come from Instagram's alt text, else the start of the caption cut at a word.
- If the Instagram connection ever lapses (Behold emails Jared to reconnect), the endpoint answers empty and the home-page section hides itself.
